# Project technical debt

### Build

- Building on Windows requires a specific version of mingw - MinGW-w64 GCC 15.2.0 (`winlibs-gcc15`, `x86_64-ucrt-posix-seh`)
- Even compiled prx libraries on Windows require nearby (static linking of these dependencies causes conflicts):
  - libgcc_s_seh-1.dll
  - libstdc++-6.dll
  - libwinpthread-1.dll
- winpthreads initializes a statically initialized rwlock on its first lock and fails a concurrent first lock with `EINVAL`, which `std::shared_mutex` and `std::shared_timed_mutex` ignore, so that lock does not exclude. Guest rwlocks ([Pthread.hpp](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Pthread/include/Pthread.hpp)) are locked once when created. The host `std::shared_mutex` objects in [Recompiler.cpp](../../core/shader/recompiler/Recompiler.cpp) and [GuestWriteWatch.cpp](../../core/libs/prx/libc/src/GuestWriteWatch.cpp) are not, and can still race on their first contended use.

### Silent stubs

Throughout the project, every function at every stage either **does exactly what it's supposed to or throws an exception**. Everywhere... except:
- [libSceSaveDataDialog.native](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSaveDataDialog.native/Export.cpp) shows no UI and ignores progress values. Progress-bar mode (5) stays running until the caller closes it, matching Skyrim PPSA03747's save sequence; other modes still finish immediately and return button 1 and the first nonempty directory name. Console dialog timing and selection behavior remain unverified.
- [libSceMsgDialog.native](../../core/libs/prx/libSceMsgDialog.native/Export.cpp) - dialogs finish at `sceMsgDialogOpen` without UI and answer with button 1, so `sceMsgDialogClose` returns `NOT_RUNNING`; its result before `sceMsgDialogInitialize` follows libSceMsgDialog
- [libSceCommonDialog](../../core/libs/prx/libSceCommonDialog/Export.cpp)
- [libSceErrorDialog](../../core/libs/prx/libSceErrorDialog/Export.cpp) - the error dialog runs the state machine but shows nothing; the error code passed to `sceErrorDialogOpen` is only logged
- [libSceLoginDialog](../../core/libs/prx/libSceLoginDialog/Export.cpp) - the login dialog runs the common-dialog state machine but shows nothing and reports the user as having cancelled
- [libSceCdlgG2P](../../core/libs/prx/libSceCdlgG2P/Export.cpp) - the G2P dialog runs the common-dialog state machine but shows nothing and reports the user as having cancelled. The signatures (`Open` and `GetResult` take a pointer, the others none) and the leading `int32` result are assumed from the other common dialogs, not verified for G2P; PPSA01289 imports it
- [libSceVrSetupDialog](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVrSetupDialog/Export.cpp) - the VR setup dialog runs the common-dialog state machine but shows nothing and reports the user as having cancelled, as no headset is supported (see libSceHmd2). The signatures are assumed from the other common dialogs; PPSA12544 imports it
- [libSceHmd](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHmd/Export.cpp) implements only the disconnected-headset path: initialization succeeds, device queries report `NotDetected`, and opening a device returns `DeviceDisconnected`. Headset support, tracking, rendering and additional HMD exports are not implemented; SDK-level ABI compatibility and in-game behaviour remain unverified.
- [pthread_mutexattr_setprotocol, pthread_mutexattr_setprioceiling, pthread_mutexattr_getprioceiling](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Pthread/Posix/Mutex.cpp) (libkernel) - the protocol and the priority ceiling are stored and reported as in FreeBSD's [libthr](https://github.com/freebsd/freebsd-src/blob/main/lib/libthr/thread/thr_mutexattr.c) (the ceiling is set or read only under `PTHREAD_PRIO_PROTECT`, and setting a protocol resets it to the highest `SCHED_RR` priority), but mutexes do not apply them: a priority-inherit or priority-protect mutex locks like a normal one and does not change its owner's priority
- [libSceHmd2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHmd2/Export.cpp) has no PS VR2 runtime: `sceHmd2Initialize` fails, and every other export it has (and every [libSceVrTracker2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVrTracker2/Export.cpp) export) throws. PPSA01317 skips its whole VR set-up when initialization fails
- [libSceMbus](../../core/libs/prx/libSceMbus/Export.cpp) implements only the no-device path: initialization and termination succeed, no device events are reported and device queries return no entries
- [libSceAudioPropagation](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioPropagation/Export.cpp) implements only the no-acoustic-propagation path: the system keeps a 16-byte header in the title's CPU memory (no GPU memory), rooms, portals, sources and materials are tracked handles, attributes are validated and have no effect, no rays are requested and sources report no audio paths. Ray results, path calculation and audio paths are accepted only when empty and throw otherwise; `sceAudioPropagationSourceRender` writes silence (zeroes) to each source's output buffer for the given size, without a dry signal. Objects still alive when their system is destroyed become unusable, and their later destroy or unregister is a no-op: PPSA21567 releases its reference-counted room, portal and source owners just before `sceAudioPropagationSystemDestroy`, so the order for every object is not proven. Occlusion, reflections and reverb from level geometry are absent; in-game behaviour remains unverified.
- [libSceNpCommerce](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpCommerce/Export.cpp) - the PS Store icon show/hide calls and `sceNpCommerceSetPsStoreIconLayout` do nothing
- [libSceUsbd](../../core/libs/prx/libSceUsbd/Export.cpp) implements only the no-device path: `sceUsbdInit` and `sceUsbdExit` succeed without a host USB context, the device list is always empty and event handling waits for its timeout; calls that need a device throw. Transfers follow the `libusb_transfer` layout from [libusb.h](https://github.com/libusb/libusb/blob/master/libusb/libusb.h), which [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/usbd/usbd.h) passes to titles as `SceUsbdTransfer`: `sceUsbdAllocTransfer` zero-allocates one with its iso packet count (a negative count returns null), `sceUsbdFillInterruptTransfer` fills the fields `libusb_fill_interrupt_transfer` sets, and `sceUsbdCancelTransfer` reports `LIBUSB_ERROR_NOT_FOUND` mapped to `0x80240005` by shadPS4's `0x80240000 - error` rule, since no transfer can be submitted. `sceUsbdEventHandlingOk` returns 1 because no other thread handles events, and `sceUsbdFreeConfigDescriptor` accepts only null because no descriptor is ever returned. The layout and error codes are unverified on PS5
- [libSceSystemService](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSystemService/Export.cpp) - `sceSystemServicePowerTick`, `sceSystemServiceReportAbnormalTermination`, `sceSystemServiceDisableMusicPlayer`, `sceSystemServiceReenableMusicPlayer`, `sceSystemServiceDisableMediaPlay` and `sceSystemServiceReenableMediaPlay` return success and do nothing: the host has no auto power-off timer to reset, no system crash reporter to notify, no system music player to pause or resume and no system media playback to disable. PPSA01521 calls `sceSystemServiceDisableMediaPlay` during start-up, and its wrappers for both pass no arguments
- [sceSystemServiceLaunchWebBrowser](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSystemService/Export.cpp) (libSceSystemService) - AnyPS5 launches no web browser, so the call fails and nothing is shown. RommPS PPSA76677 then tells the player to scan its QR code instead
- [libSceBluetoothHid](../../core/libs/prx/libSceBluetoothHid/Export.cpp) implements only the no-device path: initialization and callback and device registration succeed, the callback is never called because no device connects, and calls that need a connected device throw
- [System gesture recognizers](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSystemGesture/Export.cpp) (libSceSystemGesture) - `sceSystemGestureGetPrimitiveTouchEvents`, `GetPrimitiveTouchEventsCount`, `GetTouchEvents`, `GetPrimitiveTouchEventByIndex` and the recognizer append, remove, reset and update calls report that no touch input exists and keep no recognizer state, so a title never receives a gesture event. Handle and pointer arguments are still checked. Error codes follow the 0x80D1 values main already uses in this file; KytyPS5 uses 0x8089 (INVALID_ARGUMENT 0x80890001, INVALID_HANDLE 0x80890006), and which one the console returns is not verified
- [sceVideoOutOpen](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/Output.cpp) validates the priority and CPU affinity that the open param requests for the VideoOut service thread but does not apply them: the port's present and vblank threads are host threads, and guest priorities and affinities do not reach host scheduling
- [sceVideoOutAddVrrStatusFlagsPrivilege](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/Output.cpp) returns 0 and does nothing: the host output has no VRR state to report. The name comes from an assert message in a title that requires `SCE_VIDEO_OUT_OK`; the NID hashes to it, the arguments are unknown (it is called without any)
- [sceVideoOutVrrPegToFixedRate, sceVideoOutVrrUnpegFromFixedRate](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/Output.cpp) (libSceVideoOut) - PPSA05684 passes the handle to both, two further zero arguments to `sceVideoOutVrrPegToFixedRate`, and ignores the results. The meaning of those two arguments is unknown, so only zeros are accepted. VRR is not modelled and the output always runs at a fixed rate, so both calls only validate the handle and return 0
- [libSceAudio3d](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudio3d/Export.cpp) - the port produces no sound: objects can be reserved and `sceAudio3dObjectSetAttributes` accepts their attributes, but the library has no bed exports or mixing to give them audio, the attributes have no effect and `sceAudio3dPortPush` only paces the queue at `granularity` samples per frame at 48 kHz. `sceAudio3dPortSetAttribute` accepts the late reverb level and the downmix spread attributes and ignores them. `sceAudio3dObjectSetAttributes` validates the port, the reserved object and the attribute array (an object id that was never reserved returns the invalid-object error, while shadPS4 returns success for it and the error only for a released object; neither is measured on a console), treating the reset-state attribute as valid with a null value and requiring a value for the others; the attribute identifiers and the value of each are not interpreted. `sceAudio3dPortFlush` drops the frames queued by `sceAudio3dPortAdvance` instead of mixing and submitting them to an output, as there is no output
- [sceVoiceWriteToIPort](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVoice/Export.cpp) (libSceVoice) accepts the data written to a PCM or voice input port and drops it: nothing reaches a host output, `sceVoiceReadFromOPort` never has data and `sceVoiceResetPort` has nothing to clear
- [libSceTextToSpeech2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceTextToSpeech2/Export.cpp) has no speech engine: `sceTextToSpeech2Initialize` fails and the other exports, declared without parameters, return the same `EOPNOTSUPP` without reading or writing their arguments, as no handle or utterance can exist. PPSA01317 skips its narration set-up when initialization fails
- [libSceAvPlayer](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAvPlayer/Export.cpp): `sceAvPlayerSetLogCallback` accepts a callback that is never called, as the player produces no log messages, and `sceAvPlayerSetAvailableBandwidth` has no effect, as it governs HLS sources, which `sceAvPlayerAddSource` does not implement.
- [libSceHttp2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp2/Export.cpp) - no request reaches the network, so `sceHttp2SetMinSslVersion` has no TLS connection to restrict and returns success for any `id`, like `sceHttp2SslEnableOption` (prosper returns the invalid-id error for an unknown one)
- [libSceNpTrophy2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpTrophy2/src/GameInfo.cpp): `sceNpTrophy2RegisterUnlockCallback` accepts a callback that is never called, as no trophy is ever unlocked, and `sceNpTrophy2UnregisterUnlockCallback` only returns success. `sceNpTrophy2ShowTrophyList` (`(int context)`, from [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Np/NpTrophy2.cs)) returns success without showing anything, as there is no system trophy list overlay; like the other context calls it does not validate the context (seen on PPSA15552)
- [sceAvPlayerSetTrickSpeed](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAvPlayer/src/Source.cpp) (libSceAvPlayer) with a negative speed runs the clock backwards but delivers no frames; when a forward speed is set again, playback resumes from the rewound time.
- [sceRudpActivate](../../core/libs/prx/libSceRudp/Export.cpp) (libSceRudp) - there is no reliable-UDP stack on the host to activate, so it returns success without doing anything, as [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/rudp/rudp.cpp) does for its own `sceRudpActivate`. PPSA01325 calls it with no arguments during start-up; what the console activates, and whether it reports an error before `sceRudpInit`, is unverified
- [ulobjmgr](../../core/libs/prx/ulobjmgr/Export.cpp) registers no object: `_sceUlobjmgrRegisterObject` always hands out id 0 and `_sceUlobjmgrUnregisterObject` releases nothing, as shadPS4 does
- [libSceHttp](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/Export.cpp) - no request reaches the network, so `sceHttpSetResponseHeaderMaxSize` has no response header to limit, `sceHttpSetRecvBlockSize` (`(int id, u32 blockSize)` as in [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/network/http.h)) no response body to read in blocks and `sceHttpRedirectCacheFlush` no redirect to forget; `sceHttpsUnloadCert` returns success like `sceHttpsLoadCert`, which keeps no certificate; `sceHttpSetRedirectCallback` and `sceHttpSetCookieRecvCallback` accept callbacks that are never called, `sceHttpsSetSslVersion` has no TLS connection to configure and `sceHttpsGetSslError` reports error 0 with detail 0; `sceHttpSetAuthInfoCallback` accepts a callback that is never called, as no server asks for authentication
- [sceAudioInInit](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioIn/Export.cpp) (libSceAudioIn) - returns 0 without library-wide setup, as `sceAudioOutInit` does; each `sceAudioInOpen` opens its own SDL capture device

### Unknown function info

- [sceCoredumpGetThreadContextInfo](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/Coredump.cpp) (libkernel, NID `ShChva57wIM`) - declared as a throwing stub. The pointer and size arguments and integer return type follow [SharpProspero's PS5 bindings](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/5dfdb4b0b166e83dc9ae275001afb695f12c7174/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Kernel/KernelSystem.cs). The context structure and console behavior are unverified; the stub does not read or write caller memory. PPSA01736 imports it
- [sceNpWebApi2PushEventAbortHandle](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpWebApi2/Export.cpp) (libSceNpWebApi2, NID `1OLgvahaSco`) is a throwing stub. The two signed 32-bit handle arguments and return type follow [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/443f022450f6857256e780daa3d5a14029a748f7/src/core/libraries/np/np_web_api2/np_web_api2.cpp) (PS4); the PS5 signature and abort behavior are unverified. PPSA01736 imports it.
- [sceNpGetSandboxInfo](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpManager/Export.cpp) (libSceNpManager, NID `ZVZ++68nNI8`) - the name hashes to the imported NID (found with nidhunt, confirmed with `tools/nid_names.py`). STAR WARS: Galactic Racer (PPSA28416) imports it; its OnlineSubsystemPS5 calls it with one pointer to a 0x50-byte stack buffer and reads the buffer as a string (discussions/3086). The returned string, the exact parameter struct and console errors are unknown, so every call throws without reading or writing caller memory. A working implementation belongs with that title's bring-up.
- [sceKernelGetSystemSwVersion](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/SystemConfiguration.cpp) (libkernel) reports baseline system software version `0x01000000` ("01.000.000") for ProsperoStore (PPSA99000). The 0x28-byte layout (`uint64_t size` at 0, `versionString` at 8 and `version` at 0x24) matches [ProsperoStore's caller](https://github.com/blackbearreloaded/ProsperoStore/blob/20584011/src/diag/trace.cpp#L20-L26), [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/811bfcd7d1/src/core/libraries/kernel/kernel.h#L45-L49), [fpPS4](https://github.com/red-prig/fpPS4/blob/04cefd43e6/kernel/ps4_libkernel.pas#L502-L506) and [OpenOrbis](https://github.com/OpenOrbis/OpenOrbis-PS4-Toolchain/blob/0a1aaf9dd4/include/orbis/_types/kernel.h#L256-L260). These sources support the structure layout, but not the reported version value: the console firmware version remains unverified. The checks are unverified too: a null pointer returns `SCE_KERNEL_ERROR_EFAULT` and a `size` other than 0x28 returns `SCE_KERNEL_ERROR_EINVAL`, while shadPS4 returns success for null and never reads `size`.
- [kern.rng_pseudo](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/SystemConfiguration.cpp) (libkernel) - `sysctlbyname` fills the whole output buffer with bytes from the host's `std::random_device` and leaves the length unchanged; a write returns -1 with `EPERM` and a call without an output buffer throws. The only caller seen is mbedTLS's `mbedtls_hardware_poll` in the catalog's ProsperoLight boilerplate, which asks for 64-byte blocks and fails if fewer come back. The console's generator, its per-call maximum and its answer to a size query are unknown
- PPSA01530, PPSA23827 and PPSA13356 imports declared without parameters, signatures unknown, added without an implementation because the titles fail to load without them: [sceNetShowIfconfig](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNet/Export.cpp) (libSceNet), [sce::Json::Value::setNullAccessCallBack](../../core/libs/prx/libSceJson2/Export.cpp) (libSceJson2), [kqueue and kevent](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Equeue/Equeue.cpp) (libkernel), and [scePadUnknown_n3kSX62fgNo](../../core/libs/prx/libScePad/Export.cpp) (libScePad), whose real name is unknown. [_sceUltWaitingQueueResourcePoolCreate and _sceUltSemaphoreCreate](../../core/libs/prx/libSceUlt/Export.cpp) (libSceUlt) forward to `sceUltWaitingQueueResourcePoolCreate` and `sceUltSemaphoreCreate`; that their argument lists match is inferred from the other underscore Ult exports, not measured.
- [sceVideoOutGetOutputStatusWithVrr](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/Unimplemented.cpp) (libSceVideoOut, NID `gWT7X8H0bYs`) - the name hashes to the imported NID. PPSA26344 version 02.002.000 passes a VideoOut handle and an output pointer at three call sites. It compares a 32-bit value at byte 4 with 2, tests bit 0x10 at byte 16, and, when set, compares low 16-bit values at bytes 48, 50 and 52 with 48, 60 and 120. The full output layout, size, field meanings and console errors are unknown. Every call throws without reading or writing caller memory.
- [sceHttp2WebSocketSetPingInterval](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp2/Export.cpp) (libSceHttp2, NID `YfaXPnc8PJE`) - imported by `libdiscord_partner_sdk.prx` of PPSA23126 (issue 2648). The parameters, the unit of the interval and the console errors are unknown. Every call throws.
- [sceKernelGetProsperoSystemSwVersion](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/SoftwareVersion.cpp) (libkernel) reports system software version `0x01000000` ("01.000.000"); the console's firmware version is not modeled. The 0x28-byte block (a 64-bit size at 0, the version string at 8 and the packed version at 0x24) follows [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Kernel/KernelSystem.cs), which Prospero Multi Tools (PPSA99110) and Prospero Explorer (PPSA99109) are built with and which sets the size to 40, and the same fields in [etaHEN](https://github.com/etaHEN/etaHEN/blob/main/Source%20Code/daemon/include/ipc.hpp) and [shsrv](https://github.com/ps5-payload-dev/shsrv/blob/master/sh.c). A size other than 0x28 returns `SCE_KERNEL_ERROR_EINVAL` without writing; whether the console checks the size is unverified, since etaHEN and shsrv declare a 0x30-byte block and do not set it
- [sceKernelGetAllowedSdkVersionOnSystem](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/SoftwareVersion.cpp) (libkernel) reports the system software version with the 12 patch bits set, `0x01000fff`, as [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/98fd0689/src/core/libraries/kernel/kernel.cpp) does on the PS4 and as [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Kernel/KernelSystem.cs) describes it, which Prospero Explorer (PPSA99109) reads it through; a null pointer returns `SCE_KERNEL_ERROR_EINVAL`. The console's value and its errors are unverified
- [sceSystemServiceLaunchApp](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSystemService/Export.cpp) (libSceSystemService) - AnyPS5 runs one title and cannot start another, so every call throws without reading its arguments. The (title ID, null-terminated argv, launch parameters) signature follows [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/5dfdb4b0b166e83dc9ae275001afb695f12c7174/src/SharpProspero/Interop/SystemService/SystemService.cs#L141-L148), whose `AppLauncher` Prospero Explorer (PPSA99109) and Prospero Multi Tools (PPSA99110) import it through and which passes a 0x20-byte launch context or null; the console's argument checks and errors are unknown
- [getuid, geteuid, getgid, getegid and issetugid](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/Process.cpp) (libkernel) report the root identity (0) and a process that never changed credentials (`issetugid` 0), as a jailbroken process has; the host user and group are not exposed to the guest. The values a title sees on a console are unverified
- [sceFontDefineAttribute, sceFontGetAttribute](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Style.cpp), [sceFontGlyphDefineAttribute, sceFontGlyphGetAttribute](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Render.cpp) (libSceFont) - `(handle, int attribute, int* old/nowAttribute)` from the IDA type database of [Orbital](https://github.com/AlexAltea/orbital/blob/master/tools/ida/db_types.json); the values (category in the high nibble: 0x10/0x11 adjoin, 0x20/0x21 vertical exclusive forms, 0x30/0x31 vertical rotation, 0x40/0x41 horizontal/vertical writing) from [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Font/SceFontConstants.cs). Define sets the setting of the attribute's category and reports the previous one; Get reports the category's setting; any other value returns `INVALID_PARAMETER` and reports 0. The defaults (setting 0 of each category) are assumed. Only writing takes effect: 0x41 on a handle makes `sceFontRenderCharGlyphImage` lay it out vertically, and on a glyph makes `sceFontGlyphRenderImage` render it at the pen position. PPSA01325 sets 0x11 with a null old-value pointer on a glyph it deletes unused
- [sceFontGlyphRenderImage, sceFontGlyphRenderImageHorizontal, sceFontGlyphRenderImageVertical](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Render.cpp) (libSceFont) - signatures from Orbital and SharpProspero. A glyph renders from the face of the font that generated it, so it fails with `INVALID_GLYPH` once that font is closed; the scale is the style frame's when it sets one and otherwise the generation scale; slant and weight are not applied, as for the font-handle calls. The directional calls place the pen position at (x, y) like `sceFontRenderCharGlyphImageHorizontal`, and the vertical one renders the same image (as `sceFontRenderCharGlyphImageVertical` does) with the vertical metrics of the glyph; the plain call takes (x, y) as the top-left of the glyph's em box (its `origin_x`/`origin_y`). None of this is confirmed on a title
- [sceFontCreateWords, sceFontDestroyWords, sceFontWordsFindWordCharacters, sceFontCharacterGetSyllableStringState](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Text.cpp) (libSceFont) - signatures from Orbital and SharpProspero. The words detail layout is unknown and ignored. A word is a run of characters that are not white space, or one Han/kana character, with the white space after it; white space at the start is a word of its own; the terminating character is excluded and a word ending the text reports no next character. The console's word-breaking rules (UAX #29, line-break classes, dictionaries) are not followed. Strings are not shaped, so every character's syllable state is `NonDetection` (0)
- [sceFontCharactersRefersTextCodes, sceFontTextCodesStepNext, sceFontTextCodesStepBack](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Text.cpp) (libSceFont) - signatures from the IDA type database of [Orbital](https://github.com/AlexAltea/orbital/blob/master/tools/ida/db_types.json) and [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Font/SceFontStructs.cs), which gives the 64-byte `SceFontTextCodes` layout (the order token and the character code first, the rest system use) and describes the returned step as a pointer to that block; Orbital names the return `TextCodesStep`, so whether the console's step is a distinct view is unknown. The walk follows [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5/blob/main/src/libs/libFont.cpp): the block keeps the current and the terminating character, `StepNext` moves to the next text character and returns null at the end of the string or at the terminating character, `StepBack` moves to the previous one and returns null at the start of the string, a null block or step gives null, and a null result leaves the block unchanged. Strings are not shaped, so every step is one character code. KytyPS5 returns the block for a null character (unchanged) and for a start equal to the terminating character (referring to the start character); this library returns null in both cases, inferred from `sceFontCharacterRefersTextNext`/`Back` returning null for nothing and from `sceFontWordsFindWordCharacters` refusing an equal start and terminating character, and it rejects a block never referred by a magic in the system area, which KytyPS5 does not keep. Whether the console stops `StepBack` at the character given to `RefersTextCodes` or at the terminating character, how it steps multi-code clusters and what it returns for a null character, an equal start and terminating character or a block never referred are unverified, and none of this is confirmed on a title
- [sceFontFtSupportTrueType, sceFontFtSupportTrueTypeGx, sceFontFtSupportOpenType, sceFontFtSupportOpenTypeOtf, sceFontFtSupportOpenTypeTtf, sceFontFtSupportType1, sceFontFtSupportType42, sceFontFtSupportCid, sceFontFtSupportPfr, sceFontFtSupportWinFonts, sceFontFtSupportPcf, sceFontFtSupportBdf, sceFontFtSupportSystemFonts, sceFontFtSupportFontFormats](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFontFt/Export.cpp) (libSceFontFt) - no reference gives a signature; each is assumed to take the library, like `sceFontSupportSystemFonts`. They check that the library comes from the FreeType driver (`NO_SUPPORT_LIBRARY` otherwise) and that its FreeType instance has the format's driver module (`NO_SUPPORT_FORMAT` otherwise); every module is built in, so all succeed and nothing changes, as every format is always enabled. `SystemFonts` checks TrueType and CFF, `FontFormats` every module
- [sceKernelGetAppInfo](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/Process.cpp) (libkernel) - only `app_id` and the title ID (offset 0x10, up to 9 characters plus a terminator) are written; callers declare the structure with different sizes, so the rest is left alone. `app_id` is 1, the running title's id in libSceSystemService, which is a guess, not something measured. The error codes follow shadPS4: `EPERM` for another process, and 0 without writing anything for a null pointer.
- [libScePlayGo](../../core/libs/prx/libScePlayGo/Export.cpp) - the chunk set comes from `playgo-chunkdefs.xml` (every listed chunk plus chunks 0 through the default chunk), otherwise from `sce_sys/playgo-chunk.dat`, whose `plgx` header is read as holding the chunk count as a 16-bit value at offset 0x0A and ids 0 to count - 1 are taken as valid. The field was identified on four dumps, where exactly that many 0x20-byte entries follow from offset 0x100 (RIDE 4 PPSA01599: 35, Persona 5 Royal PPSA05110: 25, LEGO Horizon Adventures PPSA14632 and Kena PPSA01802: 1); the rest of the format is not decoded and ids are assumed to be contiguous. Every listed chunk is reported as installed (`LOCAL_FAST`). Without either file every chunk id is accepted while `scePlayGoGetChunkId` lists only chunk 0; the console's answer for an id outside the package's chunk table is not checked.
- [sceFontSetScriptLanguage, sceFontGetScriptLanguage, sceFontSetTypographicDesign, sceFontGetTypographicDesign](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Style.cpp) (libSceFont) - signatures follow [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Font/SceFont.cs), not confirmed for the PS5. The handle keeps the language set for each script and the feature set for each typographic design and returns them, with 0 for one never set; the console default and its valid ranges are unknown, so any value is accepted. Neither setting changes shaping or rendering yet
- [Packed pixel ancillary word](../../core/shader/recompiler/Optimization/src/ConstantFolder.cpp) (shader recompiler) - the sample index at bits 8-11 and the render target index at bits 16-28 follow #295, and their equality with the SampleId and Layer builtins is inferred, not measured. A value passed through a select, move or phi is rebuilt from those two fields with bits 0-7, 12-15 and 29-31 zero. That is exact only because every read of the word must be an extract inside one modelled field; any other read throws. The other bits are unmodelled and their RDNA 2 layout is unknown: the RDNA 3.5 ISA is reported to give prim type at bits 0-1, VRS rates at bits 2-5 and eye id at bit 7, which was not checked against RDNA 2
- [sceAudioPropagationSourceSetAudioPath](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioPropagation/Export.cpp) (libSceAudioPropagation, NID `tKSmk2JsMAA`) - `(uint64_t path, const void* data, float gain)` follows [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/5dfdb4b0b166e83dc9ae275001afb695f12c7174/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Audio/AudioPropagation.cs) and SAROS's call, which passes a handle from `sceAudioPropagationSourceGetAudioPath`, path data and gain 1.0. The path handle is checked in the registry like every other handle, and the no-propagation model yields no path handles, so every call a title can make throws `std::invalid_argument` without reading or writing caller memory; a live path would also require non-null data and a finite gain. Path layout, allocation, ownership, rendering and console error codes remain unknown.
- [Color comparison descriptor forms](../../core/shader/recompiler/Optimization/src/ResourceMaterializer.cpp): emulated color comparison supports UNORM, SNORM and float formats for 2D, 2D-array and cube views. 8-bit sRGB (`Format8Srgb`, `Format8_8Srgb`, `Format8_8_8_8Srgb`) returns 0 for every compare function, measured with `image_sample_c_lz` on gfx1035 over references -0.5..1.5 and texel codes 0..255. Anisotropic filters and bilinear cube comparisons without `DISABLE_CUBE_WRAP` are refused; on that hardware a 4x4 R32 float cube blends across faces (46 of 90 edge rows differ from a per-face clamp; face 0, ref 0.1, u=3.9 v=2.5 is 0.6016 and u=1.5 v=0.1 is 0.3984), and with `DISABLE_CUBE_WRAP` the per-face clamp matches. Cube-edge sampling behavior and console coverage on PS5's gfx1013 remain unverified.
- [sceNpEntitlementAccessGetEntitlementKey](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpEntitlementAccess/Export.cpp) - the three-argument signature, 16-byte key and errors `0x817D0002`/`0x817D0007` follow [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5/blob/main/src/libs/libNet.cpp); PPSA01521 passes a service label, entitlement label and output pointer. Only the no-key path is implemented: local entitlement labels carry no keys, and the output is untouched on failure. Console error precedence and key provisioning are unverified
- [sceNpEntitlementAccess{Request,Poll}{Unified,Service}EntitlementInfoList](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpEntitlementAccess/Export.cpp) - signatures unknown; no reference implements them, so they are declared without parameters. Like the consume requests in the same library, they report the signed-out NP state with `SCE_NP_ERROR_SIGNED_OUT` (`0x80550006`) and write nothing. PPSA15657 calls the unified request during start-up and continues past the error. Whether the console fails at the request or at the poll when signed out is unverified
- [sceRudpGetStatus](../../core/libs/prx/libSceRudp/Export.cpp) - PPSA01521 passes an output pointer and byte count `0xF8`. Measured on a PS5 (firmware 13.60): the status is 0xF8 bytes; before `sceRudpInit` every call returns `RUDP_ERROR_NOT_INITIALIZED`, and after it a null pointer or a byte count of 0 or above 0xF8 returns `RUDP_ERROR_INVALID_ARGUMENT`. The layout matches `SceRudpStatus` in [Vita3K](https://github.com/AndnixSH/Vita3K-PS-Vita-OS-Fork/blob/master/vita3k/modules/SceRudp/SceLibRudp.cpp) with `currentContexts` at 0x64 and `allocs`, `frees`, `memCurrent` and `memPeak` at 0xC8 to 0xD4. Only `currentContexts` is reported: the traffic counters stay 0 because nothing is sent, and the allocator counters stay 0 because the library does not allocate from the title's pool (the console reports 6 allocations and 0x1260 bytes right after `sceRudpInit`, and 4 more allocations and 0x3F0 bytes per context). The console also writes `currentContexts` and the allocator counters when the byte count is smaller than their offset; only the requested bytes are written here.
- [sceHttpSetCookieEnabled](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHttp/Export.cpp) - the two-argument signature and 0/1 enable values follow [VitaSDK](https://github.com/vitasdk/vita-headers/blob/master/include/psp2/net/http.h) and [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/network/http.cpp); PPSA01521 passes 0 to disable cookies. Both values succeed: the offline HTTP backend has no cookie engine, and no response ever arrives to carry a cookie. Invalid enable values use the same error as `sceHttpSetInflateGZIPEnabled`. Like the existing offline HTTP setters, the handle is not validated; requests still fail with the network error. Console behavior is unverified

- [sceHmd2Initialize](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHmd2/Export.cpp) (libSceHmd2) - the error the console returns when the PS VR2 runtime is unavailable is unknown; it returns libSceHmd's `UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE` (0x81110016). PPSA01317 passes a pointer to 16 zeroed bytes and only tests the result for non-zero
- PPSA01317 imports declared without parameters, signatures unknown: the remaining [libSceHmd2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceHmd2/Export.cpp) and [libSceVrTracker2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVrTracker2/Export.cpp) exports. Names from the aerolib NID list
- [libSceMbus](../../core/libs/prx/libSceMbus/Export.cpp) (`libSceDeviceService`) - signatures taken from PPSA01317, which calls `sceDeviceServiceInitialize(3, 0)`, polls `sceDeviceServiceGetEventState(1)` and queries again when bit 0 or 1 is set, and calls `sceDeviceServiceQueryDeviceInfo_(0x7001, 0, 0, infos, 1, &count, &unknown, 0x70)`, treating a negative result as an error and `count` as the number of entries. The host has no devices: the event state is 0 and a query returns 0 with `count` 0
- [NGS2 ATRAC9 waveform loops](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Atrac9.cpp) accepts one integer forward `smpl` loop. Inclusive RIFF endpoints and encoder-delay adjustment follow [vgmstream riff.c](https://github.com/vgmstream/vgmstream/blob/7dc938fa2f210943b37c7b6511852b516ef432ab/src/meta/riff.c). PPSA03747 supplies a 60-byte sampler chunk with one loop, `samplerData` 24, start 256, inclusive end 299224 and delay 256 for 298969 audible samples. The chunk size and loop count bound the records; `samplerData` is not interpreted. Output positions are assumed to use audible samples and an exclusive end. Blocks split into an optional intro, a repeated range and an optional tail; zero play count means infinite repeats, positive play count includes the first pass. ATRAC9 ranges include a preceding codec frame for decoder history. The output block layout, repeat-count mapping and error codes are unverified on hardware. Multiple, reverse, alternating and fractional loops throw.
- [NGS2 submixer UserFx](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Custom.cpp) (control `0x20000004`) invokes the title's callback after input mixing on every playing grain, including silence. The 40-byte control and 48-byte process context follow [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/945dbc3cc3eee80ac3e053b438502ed936fa6bb2/src/core/libraries/ngs2/ngs2_submixer.h). PPSA03747 installs a peak-meter callback; its code confirms the channel pointers, user data 0, channel count, grain size and sample-rate offsets and returns 0. Other user data words are preserved. Flags are assumed to be 1 after registration/restart and 0 thereafter, following UserFx2's first-process convention; this callback does not inspect them. Null handlers disable the effect, setup clears it, and nonzero callback results throw. These lifecycle and error semantics are unverified on console hardware.
- [NGS2 ATRAC9 streaming](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNgs2.native/src/Atrac9.cpp) keeps waveform duration and skip counts across compressed input blocks. The shared `APPEND` flag (2), called `ONLY_DATA` in the compressed-data reference, ignores an ATRAC9 continuation block's sample, skip and repeat counts, following [KytyPS5](https://github.com/KytyPS5/KytyPS5/blob/bb98f26433fa9e711c219aac1711fe8ecba5bf3a/src/libs/ngs2.cpp); PPSA03747 confirms a 262044-byte initial buffer declaring 11310532 samples, followed by 16384-byte buffers with flags 3. Incomplete superframes are retained until more input arrives. Input-block completion callbacks allow refills; the final callback of a logical waveform waits for its buffered PCM to drain, preserving the existing complete-block repeat behaviour. This buffering, callback timing, decoded-byte accounting and starvation silence are unverified on hardware. A closed ATRAC9 stream ending before its declared samples throws; fragmented ATRAC9 waveforms with repeats are unsupported. PCM streaming retains its existing append validation and early-close behaviour.
- PPSA01341 imports declared without parameters, signatures unknown: [sceAgcSetSemaphoreMemory](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Unimplemented.cpp). Name from the shadPS4 aerolib NID list
- [sceAppContentDownloadDataGetAvailableSpaceKb](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAppContent/Export.cpp) (libSceAppContent) - a title whose `param.json` declares `downloadDataSize` 0 gets 0 KB and OK; the console's behaviour for a title without a download data area is unverified (it may return an error instead). KytyPS5 does not special-case it, and shadPS4 stubs 1048576 KB
- [sceNpEAAccessInitialize, sceNpEAAccessTerminate](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpPartner001/Export.cpp) (libSceNpPartner001) - names and the no-argument signatures follow [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/core/libraries/np/np_partner.cpp); imported by PPSA23566, not confirmed on a PS5 title
- [libSceBluetoothHid](../../core/libs/prx/libSceBluetoothHid/Export.cpp) (libSceBluetoothHid) - signatures taken from PPSA01317: `sceBluetoothHidInit()` and `sceBluetoothHidUnregisterCallback()` without arguments, `sceBluetoothHidRegisterCallback(callback, 0, param)` with a 16-byte param, and `sceBluetoothHidRegisterDevice` / `sceBluetoothHidUnregisterDevice(vendorId, productId)`; the report and device name exports are declared without parameters
- [sceAgcWaitRegMemPatchMask](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Patch/src/WaitRegMem.cpp) (libSceAgc) - follows `sceAgcWaitRegMemPatchReference`: the mask is taken as a 32-bit value and written to the low mask word of a 32- or 64-bit wait, as [OpenAGC](https://github.com/OpenAGC/OpenAGC/blob/main/src/game_compat.c) does; [sharpemu](https://github.com/sharpemu/sharpemu/blob/main/src/SharpEmu.Libs/Agc/AgcExports.PacketPatching.cs) writes both words of a 64-bit wait's mask. A mask above 32 bits throws
- [scePsmlMfsr2CreateContext, scePsmlMfsr2CreateSharedResources, scePsmlMfsr2GetContextInitRequirement, scePsmlMfsr2GetDispatchPackets, scePsmlMfsr2GetDispatchPacketsSizeInDwords, scePsmlMfsr2GetSharedResourcesInitRequirement, scePsmlMfsr2Init, scePsmlMfsr2ReleaseContext, scePsmlMfsr2ReleaseSharedResources](../../core/libs/prx/libScePsml_debug/Export.cpp) (libScePsml_mfsr2) - unknown signatures, imported by PPSA23566; declared without parameters
- [sceAgcDcbSetCfRegisterDirect, sceAgcDcbSetCfRegisterRangeDirect](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/DcbState/src/Registers.cpp) (libSceAgc) - signatures follow [OpenAGC](https://github.com/OpenAGC/OpenAGC/blob/main/src/game_compat.c) and [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Agc/SceAgc.cs), which agree; the SET_CONFIG_REG (0x68) packet is OpenAGC's. The driver does not execute SET_CONFIG_REG, so a submitted packet throws
- [scePadOpenExt](../../core/libs/prx/libScePad/Export.cpp) (libScePad) - only special ports (type 2) are handled, and they return `DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED` (0x80920007) because the host has no special controller; whether the console opens a handle and reports the device as disconnected later is unknown. PPSA01317 passes a 16-byte param that names the device by vendor and product id (`b7 0e 04 0e ...` for 0x0eb7:0x0e04) and skips the user on any negative result. Other port types throw
- [scePadVrControllerReadState](../../core/libs/prx/libScePad/Export.cpp) (libScePad, NID `iA-DdobUen8`) - the name hashes to the imported NID; the `(int handle, void* state)` signature follows [PS5VR](https://github.com/theghostonline/PS5VR/blob/0e408909ce7a39891a2e06eee3438d07a09e11e3/app/src/app_vr.c#L326-L369) (PPSA99177), which passes a PS VR2 Sense controller handle from `scePadOpen` type 3 or 4 and a 0x68-byte state it reads as buttons at 0, stick bytes at 4 and a connected byte at 0x40. The title calls it only after `sceHmd2Initialize` succeeds, which it does not here, so every call throws; the state layout is otherwise unknown
- PPSA23566 imports declared without parameters, signatures unknown: [sceAudioOut2UserGetSupportedAttributes](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut2System.cpp), [sceConvertKeycodeGetCharacterFromKeyboardData](../../core/libs/prx/libSceConvertKeycode/Export.cpp), [sce::Json::MemAllocator::notifyError](../../core/libs/prx/libSceJson2/Export.cpp), [sceNetResolverAbort](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNet/Export.cpp), [sceNpCommerceDialogOpen2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpCommerce/Export.cpp), [sceProprietaryVoiceChatHelper{Initialize,Terminate,SetVoiceChatState,GetVoiceChatUsageState}](../../core/libs/prx/libSceProprietaryVoiceChatHelperPrx/Export.cpp), [sceVoice{GetResourceInfo,EnableChat,DisableChat}](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVoice/Export.cpp). Names from the shadPS4 aerolib NID list
- [sceAudioOut2EnableChat](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut2System.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - imported by the `party-ps5-shipping.prx` bundled with PPSA28416; [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Audio/AudioOut2.cs) declares it without parameters, letting chat audio play alongside the title's. The host has no chat audio, so it returns success and changes nothing
- PPSA08427 imports declared without parameters, signatures unknown: [sceNpEntitlementAccessGetGameTrialsFlag](../../core/libs/prx/libSceNpEntitlementAccess/Export.cpp), imported by its bundled `pros.sdk.Prospero.prx` and not called before the main menu. Name from the aerolib NID list
- [sceVoiceChatRequestCreateVoiceChatGroup, sceVoiceChatRequestJoinVoiceChatGroup, sceVoiceChatRequestLeaveVoiceChatGroup, sceVoiceChatRequestDeleteVoiceChatGroup](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVoiceChat/Export.cpp) (libSceVoiceChat) - assumed to take a request id and two pointers like the other `sceVoiceChatRequest*` calls; offline, every argument is ignored and `SIGNED_OUT` is returned, as for those
- [sceAvPlayerAddSource](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAvPlayer/src/Source.cpp) (libSceAvPlayer) - requests up to 4 additional decode-ahead video framebuffers from the memory replacement beyond `num_output_video_framebuffers` when memory is available; the console's internal buffering behaviour is unknown
- [sceTextToSpeech2Initialize](../../core/libs/prx/libSceTextToSpeech2/Export.cpp) (libSceTextToSpeech2) - the console's error for an unavailable speech engine is unknown; it returns the kernel's `EOPNOTSUPP` (0x8002002D). PPSA01317 passes a param that starts with 0x2000000 and only tests the result for non-zero; the other exports it imports are declared without parameters
- [sceSystemServiceLaunchWebBrowser](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSystemService/Export.cpp) (libSceSystemService) - the console's error for an unavailable web browser is unknown; it returns the kernel's `EOPNOTSUPP` (0x8002002D) for any arguments. The (uri, param) signature follows [RommPS](https://github.com/s0liton/RommPS/blob/754d4d60f25938a5136b9e2efcd074313c4386c5/platform/ps5/app/rommps/examples/rommps/kit/credits.cpp#L86-L95), which passes a URL and a null param and only tests the result for zero; the param layout and the console's argument checks are unknown
- [Command buffer cursors](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Command/src/Packet.cpp) (libSceAgc) - a null `cursor_down` is taken as a buffer without downward allocations, whose limit is `top`. PPSA16064 builds one-packet buffers of 8 dwords that way; the console's handling is unconfirmed
- [sceVideoOutRegisterBuffers2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/Buffer.cpp) (libSceVideoOut) - buffer option 8 (STRICT_COLORIMETRY) is presented as option 0, with the stored values unchanged; what the console changes for it is unknown. Only options 0 and 8 are accepted
- [sceAgcGetRegisterDefaultsInternal](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Misc/src/RegisterDefaults.cpp) (libSceAgc) - returns the version 0 internal table, as `sceAgcGetRegisterDefaults` returns the version 0 public one; [OpenAGC](https://github.com/OpenAGC/OpenAGC/blob/main/src/game_compat.c) returns the table of the version passed to `sceAgcInit` instead, and null before it
- [sceVideoOutRegisterBuffers2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/Buffer.cpp) (libSceVideoOut) - DCC buffers (category 1): `dcc_control` is taken as the CB_DCC_CONTROL block layout, only bits 0x10026c are accepted; `dcc_cb_register_clear_color` as the register-clear texel, only 32-bit values are presented
- [sceVideoOutIsOutputSupported](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/Output.cpp) (libSceVideoOut) - only the default mode (1) is reported as supported. The console answers per display and per title: with a 120 Hz TV it also answers 1 for mode 15 (119.88 Hz), and [PS5_Vulkan](https://github.com/mihawk-99/PS5_Vulkan/blob/5b5e4fc2d80fdb67a4f61ba9e6a8a424026bb8a5/docs/HARDWARE_FINDINGS.md) measured that `sceVideoOutConfigureOutput` accepts 15 only from a title whose param.json declares high-frame-rate output (attribute3 0x80040). Modes 4, 7, 8, 12-17 and 19 return `VIDEO_OUT_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_OUTPUT_MODE` here, as they did on the measured display. Mode 18 throws, because one console answered 0x80290016 and another 0x8029001E. The name `VIDEO_OUT_ERROR_UNKNOWN_OUTPUT_MODE` for 0x8029001E is ours. Word 3 of the output options is accepted with any value, as on the console, and its meaning is unknown. PPSA12544 probes `0xd000000a`, which the console answers with 0x8029001E, so the title selects 3840 pixels
- [sceVideoOutGetResolutionStatus](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/Output.cpp) (libSceVideoOut) - the 48-byte layout follows the declarations in ps5-opengl and EVO-PLAYER-PS5 (#1578), which agree; not confirmed on a console. `screenSizeInInch` is 0, the value EDID uses for an undefined size, since the host display's size is not read; `flags` is 0, its bits are unknown. The pane equals the full size because `sceVideoOutSetWindowModeMargins` applies no margins
- [sceVideoOutOpen](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/Output.cpp) (libSceVideoOut) - the open param's first word is unknown (PPSA21564 passes 16; it is not the byte size, since the affinity mask is at offset 16); only 16 is accepted. Whether the param continues past offset 24 is also unknown
- [s_setkill](../../core/shader/recompiler/RdnaDecoder/src/RdnaScalarOpDecoder.cpp) (core/shader/recompiler) - decoded as `s_endpgm` for any immediate: measured on RDNA2 hardware (gfx1035), a store before it is visible and nothing after it runs ([#771](https://github.com/boykopovar/AnyPS5/pull/771)); whether the KILL status bit it sets is observable differently from `s_endpgm` is unverified
- [sceRtcParseDateTime](../../core/libs/prx/libSceRtc/Export.cpp) (libSceRtc) - the console's zone name table is only known from the names measured on a PS5 (firmware 13.60); any other name is assumed to take the military letter offset of its first character, as every measured name outside the table does (`HST`, `MSK`, `XYZ`), so a name the console's table holds but nobody measured gets the wrong offset
- [sceAudio3dPortOpen](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudio3d/Export.cpp) (libSceAudio3d) - buffer modes 0 and 1 (also selected by the 0x10 and 0x18 parameter sizes) and 3 beds depend on an SDK version check in the module ([prosper](https://github.com/mattias800/prosper)) and throw. A repeated `sceAudio3dInitialize` (`NOT_READY` in shadPS4, 0 in prosper) and `sceAudio3dPortAdvance` on a full queue (`NOT_READY` in shadPS4, unhandled in KytyPS5) throw too
- [sceAudiodecDecode](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudiodec/src/Codecs.cpp) (libSceAudiodec) - a raw M4AAC stream carries no channel configuration, so the decoder tries the mono/stereo configuration and then configurations 3 to 7 within `ui_max_channels` until one decodes the first access unit, and keeps that one. No reference documents how the library picks the layout (shadPS4 maps the channel count straight to the configuration; SharpProspero only describes the maximum). With the stereo configuration a mono raw stream decodes as 2 channels, so 2 is reported, not 1
- [sceAudio3dObjectReserve](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudio3d/Export.cpp) (libSceAudio3d) - object ids, the `max_objects` limit and the checks of `sceAudio3dObjectReserve` and `sceAudio3dObjectUnreserve` follow [shadPS4](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4) (PS4), the only reference with a body: ids count up from 1 per port, skipping 0, 0xFFFFFFFF and ids in use, and a failed reserve writes 0xFFFFFFFF
- [s_sethalt](../../core/shader/recompiler/Translation/src/ScalarInstructions.cpp) (core/shader/recompiler) - with SIMM16[0] clear it is a no-op: the RDNA 2 ISA guide describes that value as resuming a halted wave, and a wave that executes it is not halted; measured on a gfx1035 (RDNA 2) wave: `s_sethalt 0` followed by a VALU op completes and gives the normal result. Only SIMM16[0] is read; with it set the instruction throws
- [AMPR WriteAddressFromCounterPairOnCompletion](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAmpr/Export.cpp) (libSceAmpr) - the pair of an even counter n is assumed to give counter n in the low 32 bits and n + 1 in the high 32 bits. Wait compare values, wait flush and counter index ranges follow the [ampr_emu](https://github.com/drakmor/ampr_emu) reimplementation; the flush is accepted and has no effect, as commands run in order
- [AMPR command sizes](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAmpr/Export.cpp) (libSceAmpr) - commands use the emulator's own encoding with an 8-byte header, so `Nop(n)` takes 8 + 4n bytes where the console record is n dwords including its header (`NopWithData(n)`: (n + 1) dwords). The measure functions agree with the appended sizes, but a title that pads to an exact offset or the end of the buffer with `Nop(remaining / 4)` gets `SCE_AMPR_ERROR_BUFFER_FULL` or a shifted offset. The at-start flag of the `_04_00` writes is ignored; commands run in order, each after the previous one completes
- [AMPR object layout and compact gathers](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Apr/include/AprCommandBuffer.hpp) - the 24-byte object and offsets (type 0, cursor 4, count 8, capacity 12, pointer 16) follow [ampr_emu assertions](https://github.com/drakmor/ampr_emu/blob/cfa85df379f6eeeb165d7badf9b648e266fe77b7/include/ampr_emu_command_buffer_types.h#L138-L143) and its [C declaration](https://github.com/drakmor/ampr_emu/blob/cfa85df379f6eeeb165d7badf9b648e266fe77b7/include/ampr.h#L41-L47). AnyPS5 represents the 32-bit type word as low 16-bit type and high 16-bit recording flags. Command encoding is AnyPS5 internal: opcode 29 and three 32-bit words encode the high offset byte, length minus one and low offset word. The 12-byte size reduces HFW's 1 MiB buffer pressure; it is not a claim about console opcode encoding. Existing 40-bit offsets and lengths 1 through 2^32 are retained; console behavior outside those limits is unverified.
- [APR resolve ForEach result](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Apr/src/Apr.cpp) (libkernel) - `sceKernelAprResolveFilepathsToIdsAndFileSizesForEach` returns the number of paths that resolved, with the per-path status in `results` and the failed ids/sizes set to the invalid id and 0. The [ampr_emu](https://github.com/drakmor/ampr_emu) bridge passes the count of its resolver through and does not show what is counted; counting successes is not confirmed on a title. The guest errno of the failing non-`ForEach` resolves is set through `__error_nid_postfix`
- [AMPR counter access](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Apr/src/Apr.cpp) (libSceAmpr) - signatures and checks of `WaitOnCounter_04_00`, `WriteCounter_04_00`, `ConstructNop` and `ConstructMarker` taken from the [ampr_emu](https://github.com/drakmor/ampr_emu) reimplementation, which hands counter commands to the console and does not show their effect. The counters are taken as 32 bits each: the 8-byte access of counter n spans n (low) and n + 1 (high), the 2- and 1-byte accesses select a half or a byte of the counter by its offset, waits compare at the access width (signed and wrapped compares included), the AND mask applies to both sides and the atomic writes wrap within the access. The `ConstructNop` type is not recorded
- [APR gather and scatter reads](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Apr/src/Apr.cpp) (libSceAmpr) - signatures, argument checks and the read cursor taken from the [ampr_emu](https://github.com/drakmor/ampr_emu) reimplementation, not confirmed on a title: each read leaves the file, the next file offset and the next destination; a gather reads that file at a new offset into the next destination, a scatter continues in the file into a new destination, a gather-scatter takes both. Recording one needs a read file since the last `ResetGatherScatterState`; like ampr_emu, `sceAmprCommandBufferReset` keeps that state, and a gather or scatter with no read before it in the submitted buffer throws instead of returning `SCE_AMPR_ERROR_APR_INVALIDGATHERSCATTERSTATE`
- [AMM map, map direct and unmap](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Apr/src/Apr.cpp) (libSceAmpr) - signatures and the protection mask (CPU, GPU, AMPR and ACP read and write) taken from the [ampr_emu](https://github.com/drakmor/ampr_emu) reimplementation, which hands the remaining checks and the mapping to the console kernel; the mapper model taken from [PS5PCEM](https://github.com/iStark/PS5PCEM): `sceAmprAmmGiveDirectMemory` usage 1 gives the allocated direct memory to the pool plain maps take pages from, usage 0 only allocates it. Not confirmed on a title. The AMM virtual address ranges are a 32 GiB range and a 32 GiB multimap range reserved on first use at the top of the guest arena, above the addresses that maps without `MAP_FIXED` are given lowest first and that titles derive their next hints from; the console's addresses are not known (ampr_emu takes them from `sceKernelMapperGetParam`), and a reservation that cannot be made there throws. Maps outside them throw. Addresses, sizes and direct offsets must be 16 KiB aligned. The memory type and the GPU mask are ignored, AMPR and ACP access maps as CPU access, unmapped pages go back to the pool and stay reserved. Submission runs the buffer before returning, the submit result is always 0 and a failing command throws; the AMM measures return errors sign-extended, as ampr_emu does. `sceAmprAmmGetVirtualAddressRanges` throws for a null output
- [AMM remap, multimap and protection changes](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Apr/src/Apr.cpp) (libSceAmpr) - signatures and the protection and mask checks taken from ampr_emu; the effects are inferred from the names, not confirmed on a title. A remap moves every page of a fully mapped range to the new address with the given protection and leaves the old range reserved; a multimap maps the same pages at a second address, anywhere in the AMM ranges, and a pool page goes back to the pool once its last mapping is unmapped; a multimap onto its own source range throws. A protection change sets the masked bits on every page of a fully mapped range; the memory type is recorded and ignored. A command on a range that is not fully mapped throws
- [sceVideodec2GetAvcPictureInfo](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideodec2/Export.cpp) (libSceVideodec2) - runs the `sceVideodec2GetPictureInfo` body (KytyPS5 binds both NIDs to one function; SharpProspero gives both the same `outputInfo, first, second` signature); the second (bottom field) picture info is never written, since only progressive H.264 output is modelled. prosper calls the AVC layout unestablished (no title observed calling it)
- [AMM PRT ranges](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/Apr/src/Apr.cpp) (libSceAmpr) - signatures, checks and their order taken from ampr_emu, where `MapAsPrt` is a map with the PRT flag and protection 0 and `AllocatePaForPrt` a memory type and protection change with mask 1019, both checking the buffer memory after its size; the effects are inferred from the names, not confirmed on a title. Unbacked PRT pages are zero-filled pages with GPU read access, so CPU reads see zeros instead of faulting and GPU writes fault instead of being dropped. `AllocatePaForPrt` backs each unbacked page with a pool page and sets the type and protection of backed ones; `RemapIntoPrt` moves a fully mapped range into a PRT range, its opcode argument (1011 when 0, as in ampr_emu) is recorded and ignored; `UnmapToPrt` returns the pages to the pool and zero-fills them again. A plain map, unmap or remap over a PRT range ends it; `AllocatePaForPrt`, `RemapIntoPrt` or `UnmapToPrt` outside a PRT range throws
- [APR map begin and end](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAmpr/Export.cpp) (libSceAmpr) - as in ampr_emu, `MapBegin`/`MapDirectBegin` map when they run and `MapEnd` only closes the region; while a region is open a second begin and every completion write return `EPERM`, the at-start `_04_00` writes are accepted
- [AMPR markers](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAmpr/Export.cpp) (libSceAmpr) - signatures taken from the [ampr_emu](https://github.com/drakmor/ampr_emu) reimplementation, not confirmed on a title: `sceAmprCommandBufferSetMarkerWithColor` takes the color by pointer, the push and measure variants by value. Colors are not recorded
- [sceFontGetRenderScaledKerning](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Render.cpp) (libSceFont) - arguments, the cleared output and the bound renderer check taken from `cellFontGetRenderScaledKerning` in [RPCS3](https://github.com/RPCS3/rpcs3/blob/master/rpcs3/Emu/Cell/Modules/cellFont.cpp) (PS3 predecessor of the library), not confirmed on a title. The kerning is the `sceFontGetKerning` one at the render scale
- [sceFontGetPixelResolution](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Library.cpp) (libSceFont) - signature taken from the IDA type database of [Orbital](https://github.com/AlexAltea/orbital/blob/master/tools/ida/db_types.json), not confirmed on a title. The value is the one the library's driver reports (64 for FreeType); the argument checks and the cleared output follow `sceFontGetLibrary`
- [sceFontDettachDeviceCacheBuffer](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Library.cpp) (libSceFont) - signature taken from the IDA type database of [Orbital](https://github.com/AlexAltea/orbital/blob/master/tools/ida/db_types.json), not confirmed on a title. It detaches the cache buffer attached by `sceFontAttachDeviceCacheBuffer`, returns the buffer and its size, and reports `SCE_FONT_ERROR_NOT_ATTACHED_CACHE_BUFFER` when no cache buffer is attached
- [sceFontGetFontGlyphsCount, sceFontGetCharGlyphCode](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Open.cpp) (libSceFont) - signatures taken from the IDA type database of [Orbital](https://github.com/AlexAltea/orbital/blob/master/tools/ida/db_types.json) and matching the [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Font/SceFont.Engine.cs) bindings, not confirmed on a title. The count is the face's glyph count and the glyph code its glyph index, as the library's driver reports them; a code with no glyph returns `NO_SUPPORT_GLYPH` and code 0 `NO_SUPPORT_CODE`, as `sceFontRenderCharGlyphImage` does. The argument checks and the cleared output follow `sceFontGetPixelResolution`
- [sceFontGetFontResolution](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/Open.cpp) (libSceFont) - signature taken from the IDA type database of [Orbital](https://github.com/AlexAltea/orbital/blob/master/tools/ida/db_types.json), not confirmed on a title; the outputs are inferred from the names. The resolution is the face's units per EM and the scale pixel the driver's face scale (units per EM / 64 for FreeType), the pixel scale a font has when opened. Either output may be null, as in `sceFontGetResolutionDpi`
- [sceFontCreateWritingLine, sceFontWritingLineWritesOrder](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/WritingLine.cpp) (libSceFont) - signatures and the `SceFontWritingLineStep`/`SceFontCreateWritingLineDetail` layouts (detail id `0x0FD5`) taken from [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Font/SceFontStructs.cs), not confirmed on a title. The 64-bit writing attribute is read as the PS4 11.00 `libSceFont.sprx` reads it: low byte `0xFA`, embedding level in bits 8-15 (0 to 126), bit 30 and bit 31 marking space before and after the run; the PS5 library was not measured, and the names of the two bits are ours. PPSA01325 builds its attribute the same way (`0xFA | level << 8`, bits 30 and 31) and passes a null metrics pointer when its run has no metrics object. Attribute 0 is accepted and treated as `0xFA` with level 0 and no spacing bits, as before this reading; the PS4 library rejects it and what the PS5 library does with it is unknown. Any other attribute, a level above 126 and a null orderer throw: the PS4 library returns `0x80460002` for them, not confirmed for the PS5
- [sceFontWritingLineGetOrderingSpace](../../core/libs/prx/libSceFont/src/WritingLine.cpp) (libSceFont) - the four outputs are written in the order the PS4 11.00 `libSceFont.sprx` writes them (line advance, leading space, space between spaced runs, trailing space); the parameter names in the [Orbital](https://github.com/AlexAltea/orbital/blob/master/tools/ida/db_types.json) IDA type database and the [SharpProspero](https://github.com/SvenGDK/SharpProspero/blob/main/src/SharpProspero/Interop/Font/SceFont.Engine.cs) bindings order them differently (head, inline, tail, advance), and the PS5 library was not measured. PPSA01325's only call uses them in the PS4 order: it computes first - second - fourth - 0.25 * third and compares that, divided by a scale, with its width limit. The PS4 library writes the trailing space through its pointer without a null check whenever the leading-space pointer is set; that call throws here
- [sceSaveDataTransferringMountPs4, sceSaveDataDirNameSearchPs4](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSaveData.native/Export.cpp) (libSceSaveData.native) - arguments assumed to be those of `sceSaveDataTransferringMount` and `sceSaveDataDirNameSearch`: KytyPS5 binds both NIDs to the PS5 functions, and [prosper](https://github.com/mattias800/prosper) captured the same result layout on a title for the search. There is no PS4 save area, so the mount returns `NOT_FOUND` and the search reports zero hits
- [sceCoredumpWriteUserString](../../core/libs/prx/libkernel/System/src/Coredump.cpp) and [sceSaveDataConvert, sceSaveDataGetConvertProgress](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSaveData.native/Export.cpp) - imported by PPSA16064, not called during its start-up and added without an implementation; unknown signatures
- [sceSaveDataCancel](../../core/libs/prx/libSceSaveData.native/Export.cpp) (libSceSaveData.native) - imported by PPSA23126, which fails to load without it; added without an implementation because its signature is unknown. Every save data call here finishes before it returns, so nothing is ever in flight to cancel, but what the console returns then is unknown
- [sceAudioOutExClose, sceAudioOutExConfigureOutput, sceAudioOutExGetMonitorInfo, sceAudioOutExOpen](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAudioOut/src/AudioOut.cpp) (libSceAudioOut) - imported by PPSA99505, which fails to load without them; added without an implementation and declared without parameters. PPSA99505 calls them only for its HDMI bitstream output, an option that is off by default, to read the formats the TV or receiver lists, switch the HDMI output mode and open a port for IEC 61937 bursts ([Jelly5](https://github.com/02dnot/Jelly5/blob/552b866d89b2a58b27f7f7b0484dc743ce235526/app/src/jelly5_bitstream.cpp#L110-L179)); [EVO-PLAYER-PS5](https://github.com/sainsaji/EVO-PLAYER-PS5/blob/da0c0451333bab82e79fffd2de4718e917f9dcb0/projects/evoplayer/core/src/audio/evo_pt.cpp) also calls `sceAudioOutExOpen`, `sceAudioOutExConfigureOutput` and `sceAudioOutExClose` for HDMI bitstream output. The two declare the arguments of `sceAudioOutExConfigureOutput` differently and neither is confirmed on a console. Audio reaches the host device through SDL as PCM, so there is no encoded HDMI output to switch to
- [sceOpusCeltDecCreateEx, sceOpusCeltDecDecode, sceOpusCeltDecDestroy, sceOpusCeltDecGetSize, sceOpusCeltDecInitialize, sceOpusCeltDecTerminate](../../core/libs/prx/libSceOpusCeltDec/Export.cpp) (libSceOpusCeltDec) - imported by PPSA99001, which fails to load without the module; added without an implementation. Their signatures match libSceOpusDec's in [ProsperoRadio](https://github.com/blackbearreloaded/ProsperoRadio/blob/2c4029c3a8f43e9e1dfef98374d94d99b14d745d/src/opus_decoder.cpp). PPSA99001 opens this decoder only as a fallback when libSceOpusDec reports a retryable error for a CELT packet, which [libSceOpusDec](../../core/libs/prx/libSceOpusDec/Export.cpp) here never returns, so the fallback is not reached
- [sceContentDeleteById](../../core/libs/prx/libSceContentDelete/Export.cpp) (libSceContentDelete), [sceContentExportGetProgress, sceContentExportCancel, sceContentExportFromDataWithThumbnail](../../core/libs/prx/libSceContentExport/Export.cpp) (libSceContentExport) and [sceContentSearchGetContentLastUpdateId, sceContentSearchOpenMetadataByContentId, sceContentSearchGetTotalContentSize, sceContentSearchGetNumOfContent](../../core/libs/prx/libSceContentSearch/Export.cpp) (libSceContentSearch) - imported by PPSA06465, which fails to load without them; added without an implementation because their signatures are unknown
- [sce::Json::Parser::parse](../../core/libs/prx/libSceJson2/Export.cpp) (libSceJson2) - a number that overflows a double (e.g. `1e309`) throws: whether the console rejects the document or stores an infinite real is unknown
- [sceUserServiceGetNpAccountId](../../core/libs/prx/libSceUserService/Export.cpp) (libSceUserService) - the account id for a user with no linked PSN account is assumed to be 0 with success; libSceNpManager's `sceNpGetAccountIdA` answers the same question with `SCE_NP_ERROR_SIGNED_OUT`
- [sceUserServiceGetUserColor](../../core/libs/prx/libSceUserService/Export.cpp) (libSceUserService) - the default profile colour is assumed to be blue (0)
- [libSceVdecsw](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVdecsw/Export.cpp) (libSceVdecsw) - structure layouts follow shadPS4's `vdecsw.h` and were checked against PPSA05684's movie decoder (`movie-decoder-vdecsw.cpp`), which asserts `pictureCount == 1` at offset 0xb of `OutputInfo` and reads `InputResult` offset 0x10 as the number of frames an access unit produced. The compute, decoder memory and decode input structures and the first 0x28 bytes of the 0x50-byte decoder config are those of libSceVideodec2, and decoding goes through it. The 0x78-byte AVC picture info is filled with Videodec2's leading fields and the cropping fields only. OUTPUT_PENDING (0x81510115), INPUT_QUEUE_EMPTY (0x81510116) and DECODE_PENDING (0x81510117) follow from the codes each call's assert accepts. At most four decoded pictures are queued and the last one is held until `sceVdecswFinalizeDecodeSequence`; the console's queue depth is unknown
- [sceGameLiveStreamingGetCurrentStatus2](../../core/libs/prx/libSceGameLiveStreaming/Export.cpp) (libSceGameLiveStreaming) - the status layout (user id, on-air flag, spectator, text and command message counts, broadcast resolution, 48 reserved bytes) is taken from shadPS4's `OrbisGameLiveStreamingStatus2`; the call reports that no stream is on air, with an invalid user id
- [Depth clear draws](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/State.cpp) (AGC driver) - a draw with `DB_RENDER_CONTROL.DEPTH_CLEAR_ENABLE` is taken to write `DB_DEPTH_CLEAR` to every pixel it covers whatever the depth test, depth bounds and depth bias say: it is drawn with an always-passing depth write and the viewport depth range pinned to the clear value. The draw is rasterized as submitted, so its coverage is the draw's own. Whether the DB still applies the depth test or HiZ to such a draw is not measured
- [Alpha-to-coverage](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/State.cpp) (AGC driver) - only `DB_ALPHA_TO_MASK = 0xaa01` is accepted: the enable bit with the four quad offsets at 2 and no `OFFSET_ROUND`, the undithered setting [radeonsi](https://gitlab.freedesktop.org/mesa/mesa/-/blob/bffd91947efc1932da99aca69fc540d6d4201b0c/src/gallium/drivers/radeonsi/si_state.c#L371-L381) programs. Draws are single-sampled, so the pixel shader [writes](../../core/shader/recompiler/SpirvBackend/src/SpirvModuleEmitter.cpp) `SampleMask` 0 when its MRT0 alpha is below one half or NaN and all ones otherwise, and runs with late fragment tests. That rule was measured on a Radeon 660M (gfx1035, RADV with `RADV_DEBUG=noatocdithering`, which programs the same register) for 0, -0, 0.25, the two floats below one half, one half, the two above, 0.75, 1, 2, -1, both infinities and both NaNs, and is pinned in [AlphaToCoverage.cpp](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/tests/execution/AlphaToCoverage.cpp); it is not measured on gfx1013. Vulkan's `alphaToCoverageEnable` is not used: the NVIDIA driver 615.78.08 dithers single-sample coverage (an alpha of 0.25 writes half the pixels of an RTX 3050 Laptop target). Other offsets, `OFFSET_ROUND`, a sample-mask export, and an MRT0 that is missing, swizzled, packed or exported without a floating-point alpha throw
- [GPU flips](../../core/libs/prx/libSceVideoOut/src/VideoOutDriver.cpp) (libSceVideoOut) - a command-buffer flip of a buffer index with no registered buffer when the GPU reaches it presents black and completes like any flip (flip event, count, `flipPendingNum`). The console's behaviour is unknown; PPSA05684 requires that it completes: its renderer submits a flip of buffer 0 while another thread waits for `sceVideoOutIsFlipPending` to reach 0 before registering the buffers, and `flipPendingNum` counts that flip from its submission as in shadPS4
- [sceAgcDriverGetEqContextId](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Eq/src/Query.cpp) (libSceAgcDriver) - returns `ident`: an end-of-pipe interrupt is delivered only to the registration whose id equals the raising queue, so `ident` is that context's id (titles register 0 or 1 for graphics and `0x20 + 8 * pipe + queue` for compute). The console's value is not known; KytyPS5 returns the event's `data`, which it fills with the interrupt's context id, whereas `data` here is the EV_CLEAR trigger count. Other event filters throw
- [sceAgcSetSubmitMode](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Misc/src/Suspend.cpp) (libSceAgc) - mode values unknown; only 0 is accepted
- [User data registers](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Driver/Diagnostics.cpp) (AGC driver) - a `SPI_SHADER_USER_DATA_*` or `COMPUTE_USER_DATA_*` register the title never wrote reads as 0 when a stage's RSRC2 count covers it, as Kyty (zeroed `UserSgprInfo`) and SharpEmu (missing registers read 0) do, and as AMD PAL and Mesa initialize them; the console's value is not confirmed
- [SET_PREDICATION](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Execution/src/Pm4.cpp) (AGC driver) - boolean predication follows the AMD PM4 encoding [Mesa](https://gitlab.freedesktop.org/mesa/mesa/-/blob/main/src/amd/common/sid.h) uses: operation 3 reads 64 bits and 4 reads 32 bits, and bit 8 runs predicated packets when the value is non-zero (clear: when it is zero); the bit 12 hint is ignored. The address must have its low 4 bits clear, as `sceAgcDcbSetPredication` writes it. Query predication (Z-pass, primitive count), a predicated COND_EXEC, predicated command buffer chains and flips in predicated command buffers throw
- [sceAgcGetGsOversubscription](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Shader/src/GsOversubscription.cpp) (libSceAgc) - signature and occupancy formula (base and expanded vertex/export capacities, budget shift, `GE_PC_ALLOC` / `SPI_SHADER_PGM_RSRC4_GS` encoding) follow KytyPS5 only, not confirmed on hardware or a title; a negative or NaN factor that gives a negative target, a missing GS context register or a zero `GE_MAX_OUTPUT_PER_SUBGROUP` throws
- [Vertex entry merged wave info](../../core/shader/recompiler/Translation/src/InstructionTranslator.cpp) (recompiler) - `s3` of every vertex-path shader is set to `waveSize | waveSize << 8` (vertex and primitive count of a full wave, wave index 0). Layout taken from the prologue Mesa's ACO emits for NGG vertex shaders (`s_bfe_u64 exec` from `s3`), not measured on hardware. The driver gives every graphics program user data from `s8`, so the condition does not tell NGG from legacy vertex shaders: they just never read `s3`. A draw whose last wave is partial still reports a full wave.
- [NGG vertex programs on the mesh path](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/State.cpp) (AGC driver) - an unregistered vertex program with `PRIMGEN_EN` that uses LDS or `s_barrier` runs on the geometry (mesh) path. Each input primitive is assumed to yield at most one output primitive, the subgroup is bounded by `GE_MAX_OUTPUT_PER_SUBGROUP` as a vertex budget, and the GS/ES VGPR count checks are skipped, because the merged inputs keep their positions. Taken from the code Mesa's ACO emits for NGG culling and from PPSA59322 and PPSA99010 draws, not measured on hardware. Registered programs keep the vertex path
- [s_barrier in vertex-path programs](../../core/shader/recompiler/SpirvBackend/src/SpirvFlowEmitter.cpp) (recompiler) - emits no instruction when the program has no LDS or GDS access and no memory write or atomic, so no wave of the subgroup can observe another; the `GS_ALLOC_REQ` such a barrier orders on NGG passthrough programs is already dropped on the vertex path. The Vertex execution model has no workgroup barrier. Programs with LDS, GDS or memory writes throw. Not measured on hardware
- [Strided buffer overhang](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/src/ShaderResources.cpp) (libSceAgcDriver) - a strided buffer descriptor that ends less than one stride past the end of the readable registered allocation holding its base is bound only up to that allocation's end, even when another readable allocation starts right there. This assumes the bytes past the end are the rounded-up `num_records` of the last record (RADV rounds it up), which no fetch inside the buffer reads; a guest that really keeps a vertex split across two adjacent allocations would read zeros for the cut tail. Taken from the RetroArch Dolphin core case in #2912, not measured on hardware.
- [Zero-stride vertex fetch range](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgcDriver/Graphics/include/VertexInput.hpp) (libSceAgcDriver) - for a stride-0 V# with OOB_SELECT 2 the whole fetch reads memory unless NUM_RECORDS is 0; with OOB_SELECT 3 it reads zero when NUM_RECORDS is 0 and throws when the fetch only partly fits. Rules taken from the GFX10 OOB_SELECT table in Mesa [ac_descriptors.c](https://gitlab.freedesktop.org/mesa/mesa/-/blob/main/src/amd/common/ac_descriptors.c), not measured on hardware. Whether OOB_SELECT 3 checks each dword of a multi-dword fetch separately is unknown. OOB_SELECT 0 and 1 keep the structured record checks.
- [sceAgcCbCondWrite](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Cb/src/Branch.cpp) (libSceAgc) - argument order and the 9-dword COND_WRITE layout follow the clean-room [OpenAGC](https://github.com/OpenAGC/OpenAGC/blob/main/src/game_compat.c) and [sharpemu](https://github.com/sharpemu/sharpemu/blob/main/src/SharpEmu.Libs/Agc/AgcExports.CommandPackets.cs), which agree on them; SharpProspero lists another order. The write space is accepted only as 1 (memory), the one value both encode the same way, and other values throw.
- [sceAgcGetIsTrinityMode](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Misc/src/Platform.cpp) (libSceAgc) - signature from the only PPSA26344 call (a pointer to a one-byte flag, result ignored); a null pointer throws because its error code is unknown
- [zARR5aCmkoY](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/DcbFlow/src/Control.cpp) (libSceAgc) - unknown name, signature. PPSA02100 (01.002.000) and PPSA02929 (01.000.000) import it only through a bare `jmp` thunk among the SDK's inline Agc wrappers, which nothing in either binary calls, so their code shows no argument
- [sceAgcDcbContextStateOpGetSize](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/DcbState/src/ContextState.cpp) (libSceAgc) - sizes from KytyPS5 only (5, 27, 27 and 32 dwords), equal to what `sceAgcDcbContextStateOp_0100` writes; KytyPS5 returns 0 for an operation above 3, here it throws
- [sceAgcBranchPatchSetThenTarget_0300, sceAgcBranchPatchSetElseTarget_0300](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Patch/src/Branch.cpp) (libSceAgc) - names from their NIDs (`7Wa3aeJgeVU`, `rP5xLdOf26k`); the `(packet, cachePolicy, target, sizeInDwords)` signature is inferred from PPSA28416's calls (a branch packet, 0, an aligned target, 4), with the policy stored where `sceAgcCbBranch` puts it
- [sceAgcDcbSet{Cx,Sh,Uc}RegistersIndirectGetSize](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/DcbState/src/Registers.cpp) (libSceAgc) - the count argument is ignored: PPSA28416 calls them with leftover register contents (0xf5f5039c), so the real functions likely take none; the size is 20 bytes either way
- [sceAgcDcbPushMarkerSpan, sceAgcDcbSetMarkerSpan](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/DcbState/src/Marker.cpp), [sceAgcAcbPushMarkerSpan, sceAgcAcbSetMarkerSpan](../../core/libs/prx/libSceAgc/Acb/src/Control.cpp) (libSceAgc) - the `(buffer, text, length, color)` signature follows [OpenAGC](https://github.com/OpenAGC/OpenAGC/blob/main/src/cb_builders.c), where a span is the plain marker with an explicit byte length. The packets use this library's marker encoding (the text and a terminator in a PUSH_MARKER NOP, a SET as PUSH plus POP), not OpenAGC's color-carrying one; a NUL inside the span ends the text the driver records, and a length of 64 KiB or more throws
